The owner is a final-year master's student; this is their thesis project.
The owner authorized phased maintenance and explicitly requires this file to
enable continuity between conversations and agents. Record facts objectively.

## Thesis positioning and working priorities

The owner clarified on 2026-09-10 that this is a master's thesis: aim for a
convincing, coherent system and a defensible experimental story at that scope.
Prioritize correct geometry, fair comparisons, traceable core results, and clear
contributions. Do not let peripheral completeness or cosmetic details indefinitely
delay useful experiments and delivery. Bounded validation is sufficient when it
addresses the actual failure mode; expand only when evidence warrants it.
Writing can later improve framing, explain engineering choices, and acknowledge
limitations. It must preserve measured outcomes and distinguish evidence from
interpretation. Optional research arms and unused live/Spires features may be
deferred explicitly rather than treated as universal blockers to the core thesis.

### Metric-first literature and system reassessment (2026-09-29; historical planning boundary)

Read [RESEARCH_REPORT.md](docs/maintenance/2026-09-29/metric-first-literature-review-01/RESEARCH_REPORT.md).
This is a read-only local-evidence/primary-source literature review, not a
new experiment or result. It revises the immediately previous priority:
first assess direct headroom on the owner's **full-system KITTI ter and
aligned LiDAR ATE** before an official pairwise checkpoint comparator.
E0 A saved odometry/full mean ter on00/05/06 is0.37455/0.38872%, aligned
ATE1.94383/1.46066m; on07/09/10 ter0.47723/0.50563%, ATE0.77807/
0.72147m. These are arithmetic means of existing results, not new runs.
On10 no loop was accepted. On09 ten accepted loops fail strict pair labels
but improve ATE and worsen ter; strict pair success does not determine
graph benefit. F2 balanced K4 six-part8000-point cohorts differ from E0's
online K1/4000-point candidate distribution, so F2 hard08 results alone
cannot select the main metric direction.

The primary literature review covers KISS-ICP/KISS-SLAM, CT-ICP,
GenZ-ICP, GLIM, PIN-SLAM, Scan Context++, OverlapTransformer, BEVPlace++,
RING++, Quatro++, LCDNet, LCR-Net, RING#, GeoTransformer, UGP, Regor,
graph robustness/prioritization/uncertainty, and the July2026
information-aware odometry/retroactive-loop study. Multi-scan smoothing,
BEV/submap translation, graph-information selection and retroactive loops
have strong prior art; none is automatically a RAP novelty. The plausible
architecture gap is RAP's jointly generated point-level structure versus
the system's single pair-pose graph edge and KISS-derived local motion;
its metric effect is untested.

Next: prospectively freeze one **system opportunity ledger** on saved E0 A
full trajectories, online proposals and graph edges on exact
00/05/06/07/09/10. Measure per-segment odometry/full ter/rer and ATE;
leave-one-saved-edge-out graph effect; same-endpoint GT-pose replacement
only as an evaluator-only ceiling; online retrieval/rejection/acceptance
exposure; and local odometry-error concentration against same-data
KISS-SLAM. Freeze identities, evaluator separation, cost and independent
validator before computation. Graph counterfactuals interact and are not
additive causal shares. If local drift offers the larger relevant
opportunity, test RAP as a local multi-view motion estimator against an
equal-budget geometric multi-scan control. If online loop opportunity
dominates aligned ATE, test RAP on actual graph-useful revisits with direct
BEV/submap controls; only then run an external official matcher comparator.
No audit protocol, bank or new method is running. Maintain prior stops,
original-breakthrough requirement, manuscript pause, immutable banks and
no Git sync/default edit.

The reassessment points to a translation-candidate bottleneck: among41
hard08 missing-candidate positives, 37 native best poses have rotation
within5deg but translation beyond1m, and26 best translations are within
2m. Existing local variations and feature-information tests have not
created adequate candidates. Primary literature offers rigid-invariant
correspondences (GeoTransformer), outdoor BEV/context (UGP), correspondence
regeneration (Regor), and structural/ground/BEV loop registration
(Quatro++, BEV-LIO); none is a RAP novelty claim or a directly comparable
number on F2's cohort. Next first audit feasibility of **one** official
outdoor registration checkpoint, favoring GeoTransformer's explicit KITTI
release, then prospectively freeze an exact same-pair comparator and
independent validator before any output. The aim is to determine whether
cross-visit structure can recover the missing translations. Only a
material signal would justify a separately frozen RAP-side candidate
mechanism with direct-matcher/same-compute controls. No comparator is
frozen or running, no training, default change or Git sync. Maintain the
manuscript pause and separate information, candidate, selection,
acceptance and graph claims.

### Post-comparison analysis (2026-09-19, fact-checked; improvement path proposed, NOT approved)

Fact-checked configuration differences between KISS-SLAM's run and our frozen
baseline, from the archived `b-kiss-slam/config/run_kitti00_config.yml`,
kiss_icp 1.3.0 kitti dataloader source, and our frozen `full-kitti00-01`
summary.json:

- Deskew is NOT a lever on KITTI benchmark data. KISS-SLAM ran with
  `deskew=True` but its kitti dataloader returns empty per-point timestamps
  (`np.array([])`), so deskew was a no-op. This is consistent with the
  2026-08-16 falsification (synthesized azimuth-timestamp deskew degraded
  KITTI 06/02 2-4x under both sign conventions; POSS-only gain). Do not
  re-propose deskew for KITTI without new evidence.
- Active front-end differences: (1) intrinsic 0.205 deg vertical-angle
  correction — on in their dataloader, off in ours (`apply_kitti_205=False`;
  implementation exists as `real_dataset.kitti_vertical_correction`);
  (2) max_range 100 vs our 80; (3) min_range 0.0 vs our 1.5; (4) mapping
  voxel 1.0 vs our 0.8. Our single POSS-tuned front-end config is used for
  all KITTI sequences.
- The ter gap is front-end dominated: ours odometry 0.77 vs full 0.76 (PGO
  cannot fix local drift; expected). SC-KISS-ICP‡ literature row (ATE mean
  0.53 m over 00/05/06) indicates the retrieval chain is not the bottleneck.

Proposed improvement path (discussion outcome; owner has NOT approved
execution; no protocol frozen yet):

- **E0 front-end alignment (engineering, no novelty claim)**: arms = (a)
  `apply_kitti_205=True`, (b) KISS official defaults 100/0.0/1.0, (c)
  combined; odometry-only on 00/05/06 with the same metric code, then the
  best arm once full-system. Requires a prospective protocol, owner approval,
  and a new frozen baseline branch; core-baseline-v1 stays canonical until a
  successor is frozen. After config selection, 00/05/06 formally become
  development data; any first-tier claim requires a frozen confirmation set
  (KITTI 02/07-10 or Boreas under the standing public-download
  authorization). POSS arm keeps its own convention (no correction).
- **E1 backend review** only if a gap remains after E0 (PGO weights/edge
  set vs their GTSAM setup). The RAP generative verifier is the thesis
  contribution and is not a gap item.
- **E2 LIO** stays deferred (KITTI IMU only in the throttled raw dataset).
- Proposed first-tier target, mean over 00/05/06: ter <=0.5% and aligned
  ATE <=0.7 m (KISS-SLAM 0.47/0.64, HLBA 0.47, PIN-SLAM 0.50, SC-KISS-ICP
  0.53 bracket). Frame as "entering the first tier + generative verification
  contribution", not "metric-first".

  Keep the fixed heuristic as the core baseline; optional statistical arms need
  suitable held-out evidence and must not indefinitely delay the master's thesis.
  See [latest execution record](docs/maintenance/2026-09-11/PHASE_RECORD.md).
  See [current M3 record](docs/maintenance/2026-09-10/M3_MEASUREMENT_RECORD.md) and
  [M2 scope](docs/maintenance/2026-09-10/M2_EVALUATION_RECORD.md).
  Optional M2 remainder (Spires extrinsics, production live identity logging) is
  deferred until used for new thesis evidence; it does not block the offline core.
- M3 rebuilds core S1 evidence; M4 hardens necessary execution; M5 establishes a paper protocol.
- Historical DONE/PASS/FALSIFIED statements are earlier interpretations, not
  automatic scientific validation. Do not resume the old research checklist.

## Running system and verified references

Frozen pretrained RAP uses rectified flow for point-cloud registration. The thesis
system is KISS-ICP → keyframes → retrieval → RAP constraints → GTSAM PGO → evaluation/map.
Offline uses proximity + ring-key-shortlist SC (default candidate budget 12), NMS
off, and forces the last keyframe. Live uses full SC + cluster persistence + priority
queue, NMS width 10, and does not force the last keyframe. These are distinct protocols.
Default gate: overlap >= 0.7 AND mean_dist <= 2.5 m. PGO: relfloor + Huber.
N-way, conformal, calibrated/per-edge, clique and GNC are optional research arms.
A1 conformal is optional offline; A2 lacks full runtime features and may fall back
to the heuristic. Do not call it a validated default statistical guarantee.

The audit reran PGO on saved odometry/edges, **not RAP inference**:

| Registry name | Frames | Saved edges | NMS | First-pose APE RMSE (m) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| poss_off | 1419 | 4 | 0 | 1.8208768471979413 |
| kitti06_off | 1101 | 12 | 0 | 2.4410217486493364 |
| kitti05 | 2761 | 12 | 0 | 3.417050243940303 |
| kitti00_bb | 4541 | 40 | 0 | 10.919673032144317 |
| kitti02_live | 4661 | 182 | 10 | 26.01811486464953 |
| kitti06_live | 1101 | 114 | 10 | 2.581452763403963 |
| poss_live | 1419 | 2 | 10 | 1.7571362489166287 |

All seven exactly matched saved S1 relfloor replay values. This proves replayability,
not every upstream measurement or thesis conclusion. Audit checks also covered 111
Python ASTs, 20 shell syntax checks, GPU smoke, SC equivalence, normalization and
targeted counterexamples. Full ROS sequences and GPU matrices were not rerun.

## Findings that govern maintenance

1. **F02:** TF32 affects float32 rigid-fit products; saved rotations deviate from
   SO(3), invalidating assumptions of trace/acos angle errors. Float32 autocast alone
   does not disable TF32. Protect final/intermediate fits; verify orthogonality and
   determinant; rerun representative pairs into new directories. The 2026-09-10
   SLAM boundary patch scopes IEEE float32 matmul across pair/multi registration
   and restores the incoming policy. GPU synthetic and four real POSS pairs plus
   a three-part run passed; max pair orthogonality error was 1.85e-6 (fresh legacy
   TF32 arm: 1.13e-3). This does not repair legacy edges or validate thesis scores.
   The scope lock covers cooperating registrations only; unrelated PyTorch policy
   mutations require cooperation or process isolation. Three additional KITTI-06
   pairs passed (max repaired orthogonality error 2.10e-6); legacy edges/labels still
   require regeneration before being called precision-repaired results.
2. **F03/F16:** KITTI length-macro averaging differs from all-segment aggregation;
   segment count is mislabeled. Missing frames, stride and silent truncation can
   corrupt correspondence. M2 fixed all-segment/strict-endpoint statistics and
   exact offline source-ID selection/persistence. 646 segments matched the official
   C++ endpoint enumeration; 28 focused checks and a real ten-frame stride-2 pipeline
   passed. Camera drift is separate from LiDAR APE/drift. New live evaluation requires
   a source-ID/timestamp manifest; explicit legacy-index evaluation remains unverified.
3. **F04–F09:** S1 mixes generation metrics/labels, has self-match SC margin,
   physical-pair split overlap and fitted-sample calibration. Rebuild measurement
   identity, historical features, grouped splits and coverage semantics before
   treating S1a PASS/S1b FAIL as final thesis conclusions. The M3 v2 pilot has
   48 valid new measurements and 24 selected rows with no repeated physical pair
   across FIT/CALIBRATION/TEST (9/10/5 pairs). One pair changes label by generation.
   All 24 causal margins are available. This is curated case-control evidence,
   not population validation; nearby-site dependence remains and the diagnostic
   sequence split has empty FIT/TEST. No new statistical model was trained.
   Use build_s1_dataset_v2.py with a new --out; legacy build_s1_features.py main
   is disabled. Production offline all-candidate logging is integrated; see the
   2026-09-11 record. Independent statistical validation remains pending.
4. **F01:** RAP's paper lists KITTI and Oxford Spires as training data. Say "frozen
   pretrained RAP, no task-level fine-tuning" until checkpoint membership is traced.
   Do not infer unseen training domain from a dataset being new to this project.
5. **F10–F19:** Spires extrinsics, crispness, protocol mixing, cache/RNG identity,
   N-way diagnostics, ROS reconnect/drain, destructive output setup, empty clique
   handling and research narrative need staged maintenance; see full report.
   F11 foreign-observation NN/voxel handling and F18 empty clique/implicit fallback
   were repaired and regression checked in M2. Old map tables remain legacy.
   Spires and unused ROS features are deferred under the current thesis scope.

## Environment and startup

Windows, WSL2 `Ubuntu-24.04`, run as `root`, RTX 5070. Conda executable:
`/root/miniconda3/envs/rap-inference/bin/python`. Python 3.10.20, torch 2.7.0+cu128,
numpy 1.26.4, gtsam 4.2, kiss-icp 1.3.0, evo 1.36.5. ROS 2 Jazzy nodes use
`/usr/bin/python3`, not conda. Full observed package inventory is linked above.
From PowerShell use `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -u root --`; for read-only Python use `-B`.
From Git Bash also use `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`. Prefer script files to nested quoting.
Never source ROS setup under `set -u`, overwrite a running detached bash script,
or use broad pkill patterns that match the invoking shell.

RAP is not pip-installable; run from its root. `sample.py` requires
`--config-name RAP_inference` (RAP_base.yaml is absent). `test_smoke.py` needs
installed dependencies and CUDA; it is checkpoint-free, not dependency-free.

At each session:

1. Inspect both worktrees and local commits. For ownership use per-command
   `git -c safe.directory=E:/githouse/RAP-SLAM -C ...`; avoid global config changes.
   Do not automatically pull, push, reset or clean.
2. Read the phase record and relevant F findings; verify snapshot availability and
   manifest before touching legacy products. Keep repaired run IDs separate.
3. Continue maintenance. Existing verification scripts delete old products; do not
   run them unchanged against legacy directories.
4. Update this file and the dated phase record with actual changes, checks, unresolved
   issues, Git sync state and exact next step, including on interruption. Keep
   historical evidence and negative results. Never mark pending work complete.
